				add_to_variable = {

				}

						}
					}
					country_event = {
						id = ww1_austriahungary.204
						days = 1
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	completion_reward = {
		set_technology = {
			armored_train = 1
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = available_industrial_concern
		show_ideas_tooltip = mavag
	}
}
